   30: function formatUciMove(fen: string, uci: string): string {
   31:   const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
   32:   if (!match) return uci;
   33:   try {
   34:     const position = new Chess(fen);
   35:     const move = position.move({
   36:       from: match[1] as Square,
   37:       to: match[2] as Square,
   38:       ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
   39:     });
   40:     return move.san;
   41:   } catch {
   42:     return uci;
   43:   }
   44: }
   45: 
   46: function formatUciPv(fen: string, pv: string[]): string {
   47:   const position = new Chess(fen);
   48:   const san: string[] = [];
   49:   for (const uci of pv) {
   50:     const match = /^([a-h][1-8])([a-h][1-8])([qrbn])?$/.exec(uci);
   51:     if (!match) break;
   52:     try {
   53:       const move = position.move({
   54:         from: match[1] as Square,
   55:         to: match[2] as Square,
   56:         ...(match[3] ? { promotion: match[3] as "q" | "r" | "b" | "n" } : {}),
   57:       });
   58:       san.push(move.san);
   59:     } catch {
   60:       break;
   61:     }
   62:   }
   63:   return san.join(" ");
   64: }

   86: function readableDate(timestamp: number): string {
   87:   if (!timestamp) return "Not opened";
   88:   const elapsed = Date.now() - timestamp;
   89:   if (elapsed < 60_000) return "Just now";
   90:   if (elapsed < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(elapsed / 60_000)}m ago`;
   91:   if (elapsed < 86_400_000) return `${Math.floor(elapsed / 3_600_000)}h ago`;
   92:   return new Date(timestamp).toLocaleDateString();
   93: }

   95: function squareName(fileIndex: number, rankIndex: number): Square {
   96:   return `${"abcdefgh"[fileIndex]}${rankIndex + 1}` as Square;
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function normalizedCandidate(value: CandidateInput, index: number): Candidate {
  100:   const { relative_score, confidence, ...candidate } = value;
  101:   return {
  102:     ...candidate,
  103:     id: candidate.id || `candidate-${index + 1}`,
  104:     source: candidate.source ?? "automatic",
  105:     relativeScore: candidate.relativeScore ?? relative_score ?? confidence ?? 0,
  106:   };
  107: }

  337:   useEffect(() => {
  338:     function onKey(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  339:       if (!session || !book || isTypingTarget(event.target)) return;
  340:       if (cropEditingId && ["ArrowLeft", "ArrowRight", "ArrowUp", "ArrowDown"].includes(event.key)) {
  341:         const candidate = (session.candidatesByPage[String(session.page)] ?? []).find((item) => item.id === cropEditingId);
  342:         if (!candidate) return;
  343:         const step = event.shiftKey ? 0.01 : 0.002;
  344:         const x = event.key === "ArrowLeft" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowRight" ? step : 0;
  345:         const y = event.key === "ArrowUp" ? -step : event.key === "ArrowDown" ? step : 0;
  346:         const next = editCrop(candidate, "move", x, y);
  347:         patchSession((current) => {
  348:           const pageKey = String(current.page);
  349:           return {
  350:             ...current,
  351:             candidatesByPage: {
  352:               ...current.candidatesByPage,
  353:               [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((item) =>
  354:                 item.id === candidate.id ? { ...item, ...next } : item),
  355:             },
  356:           };
  357:         });
  358:         event.preventDefault();
  359:         return;
  360:       }
  361:       if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") changePage(-1);
  362:       if (event.key === "ArrowRight") changePage(1);
  363:     }
  364:     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  365:     return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  366:   });

  489:       engineRef.current = null;
  490:       setEngineEnabled(false);
  491:       setEngineStatus("off");
  492:       setEngineScore(null);
  493:       setEngineMessage("");
  494:       return;
  495:     }
  496:     try {
  497:       setEngineScore(null);
  498:       setEngineMessage("");
  499:       const client = new StockfishEngine(
  500:         `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}stockfish/stockfish-19-lite-single.js`,
  501:         (status, message) => {
  502:           setEngineStatus(status);
  503:           if (status === "error") {
  504:             setEngineMessage(message || "Stockfish could not start. Check the engine assets and browser worker support.");
  505:             setEngineEnabled(false);
  506:             setEngineScore(null);
  507:             engineRef.current?.dispose();
  508:             engineRef.current = null;
  509:           }
  510:         },
  511:         setEngineScore,
  512:       );
  513:       engineRef.current = client;
  514:       setEngineEnabled(true);
  515:     } catch (cause) {
  516:       setEngineStatus("error");
  517:       setEngineMessage(`Stockfish could not start: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  518:       setEngineEnabled(false);
  519:     }
  520:   }
  521: 
  522:   function patchSession(change: Partial<BookSession> | ((current: BookSession) => BookSession)) {
  523:     setSession((current) => {
  524:       if (!current) return current;
  525:       return typeof change === "function" ? change(current) : { ...current, ...change };
  526:     });
  527:   }
  528: 
  529:   function patchBoardSession(change: Partial<StudyBoardState>) {
  530:     if (workspaceMode === "opening-explorer") {
  531:       return;
  532:     }
  533: 
  534:     if (workspaceMode === "analysis") {
  535:       setAnalysisBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
  536:     } else if (workspaceMode === "opening-prep") {
  537:       setOpeningPrepBoard((current) => ({ ...current, ...change }));
  538:     } else if (studyMode) {
  539:       setStudyBoard((current) => current ? { ...current, ...change } : current);
  540:     } else {
  541:       patchSession(change);
  542:     }
  543:   }
  544:   async function toggleStudyMode() {
  545:     if (!studyMode) {
  546:       if (!session) {
  547:         setError("Open a book before entering study mode.");
  548:         return;
  549:       }
  550:       setStudyBoard({
  551:         lineBaseFen: session.lineBaseFen,
  552:         currentFen: session.currentFen,
  553:         moves: [...session.moves],
  554:         moveIndex: session.moveIndex,
  555:         flipped: session.flipped,
  556:         coordinates: session.coordinates,
  557:       });
  558:       setStudyMode(true);
  559:     } else {
  560:       // Preserve the latest Focus Mode board when returning to normal mode.
  561:       if (studyBoard) {
  562:         patchSession({
  563:           lineBaseFen: studyBoard.lineBaseFen,
  564:           currentFen: studyBoard.currentFen,
  565:           moves: [...studyBoard.moves],
  566:           moveIndex: studyBoard.moveIndex,
  567:           flipped: studyBoard.flipped,
  568:           coordinates: studyBoard.coordinates,
  569:         });
  570:       }
  571:       setStudyMode(false);
  572:     }
  573:     setLastMove(null);
  574:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  575:     setPromotionRequest(null);
  576: 
  577:     try {
  578:       if (!document.fullscreenElement) {
  579:         await document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
  580:       } else {
  581:         await document.exitFullscreen();
  582:       }
  583:     } catch {
  584:       // Focus layout still works when browser fullscreen is blocked.
  585:     }
  586: }
  587: 
  588:   async function openBook(item: BookRecord) {
  589:     try {
  590:       const existing = sessionIndex[item.id] ?? await getSession(item.id);
  591:       const nextSession = existing ?? newSession(item.id);
  592:       const opened = { ...item, lastOpenedAt: Date.now() };
  593:       await Promise.all([putBook(opened), putSession(nextSession), setActiveBookId(opened.id)]);
  594:       setBooks((current) => [...current.filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== opened.id), opened].sort((a, b) => b.lastOpenedAt - a.lastOpenedAt));
  595:       setSessionIndex((current) => ({ ...current, [opened.id]: nextSession }));
  596:       setBook(opened);
  597:       setSession(nextSession);
  598:       setNotice(`Opened ${opened.title}`);
  599:       setError("");
  600:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  601:     } catch (cause) {
  602:       setError(`Could not open book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  603:     }
  604:   }
  605: 
  606:   async function addBook(file?: File) {
  607:     if (!file) return;
  608:     try {
  609:       if (file.size > FILE_LIMIT) throw new Error("PDFs must be 100 MB or smaller.");
  610:       const header = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 5).arrayBuffer());
  611:       if (new TextDecoder().decode(header) !== "%PDF-") throw new Error("The selected file does not contain a valid PDF header.");
  612:       const now = Date.now();
  613:       const created: BookRecord = {
  614:         id: crypto.randomUUID(),
  615:         title: file.name.replace(/\.pdf$/i, ""),
  616:         file: file.slice(0, file.size, "application/pdf"),
  617:         byteLength: file.size,
  618:         lastOpenedAt: now,
  619:         addedAt: now,
  620:       };
  621:       const blank = newSession(created.id);
  622:       await Promise.all([putBook(created), putSession(blank), setActiveBookId(created.id)]);
  623:       setBooks((current) => [created, ...current]);
  624:       setSessionIndex((current) => ({ ...current, [created.id]: blank }));
  625:       setBook(created);
  626:       setSession(blank);
  627:       setNotice("PDF added to this browser's local library.");
  628:       setError("");
  629:       if (fileInput.current) fileInput.current.value = "";
  630:     } catch (cause) {
  631:       setError(`Could not add PDF: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  632:     }
  633:   }
  634: 
  635:   async function deleteBook(item: BookRecord) {
  636:     if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${item.title}" and its saved reading progress from this browser? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
  637: 
  638:     try {
  639:       await removeBook(item.id);
  640:       await removeSession(item.id);
  641: 
  642:       setBooks((current) => current.filter((entry) => entry.id !== item.id));
  643:       setSessionIndex((current) => {
  644:         const next = { ...current };
  645:         delete next[item.id];
  646:         return next;
  647:       });
  648: 
  649:       if (book?.id === item.id) {
  650:         setBook(null);
  651:         setSession(null);
  652:         setPdf(null);
  653:       }
  654: 
  655:       setNotice(`Removed "${item.title}" from your library.`);
  656:       setError("");
  657:     } catch (cause) {
  658:       setError(`Could not remove book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  659:     }
  660:   }
  661:   async function renameBook(item: BookRecord) {
  662:     const title = window.prompt("Book title", item.title)?.trim();
  663:     if (!title || title === item.title) return;
  664:     const renamed = { ...item, title };
  665:     try {
  666:       await putBook(renamed);
  667:       setBooks((current) => current.map((entry) => entry.id === item.id ? renamed : entry));
  668:       if (book?.id === item.id) setBook(renamed);
  669:     } catch (cause) {
  670:       setError(`Could not rename book: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  671:     }
  672:   }
  673: 
  674:   function changePage(delta: number) {
  675:     if (!session) return;
  676:     patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 999_999, session.page + delta)) });
  677:   }
  678: 
  679:   function goToPage(value: number) {
  680:     if (!session || !Number.isFinite(value)) return;
  681:     patchSession({ page: Math.max(1, Math.min(session.pageCount || 1, Math.floor(value))) });
  682:   }
  683: 
  684:   function moveToPosition(fen: string): boolean {
  685:     const activeSession = session ?? newSession("analysis");
  686:     try {
  687:       const next = new Chess(fen);
  688:       setSession({
  689:         ...activeSession,
  690:         originalFen: fen,
  691:         lineBaseFen: fen,
  692:         currentFen: next.fen(),
  693:         moves: [],
  694:         moveIndex: 0,
  695:       });
  696:       setLastMove(null);
  697:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  698:       setUserArrows([]);
  699:       setNotice("Position loaded. Reset Board returns to this diagram position.");
  700:       return true;
  701:     } catch (cause) {
  702:       setError(`Invalid chess position: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  703:       return false;
  704:     }
  705:   }
  706: 
  707:   function analyzeManualPosition(fen: string): boolean {
  708:     const loaded = moveToPosition(fen);
  709:     if (!loaded) return false;
  710:     setEngineScore(null);
  711:     setEngineMessage("");
  712:     if (!engineEnabled) {
  713:       toggleEngine();
  714:     } else {
  715:       engineRef.current?.analyze(fen, engineDepth);
  716:     }
  717:     setNotice("Manual position loaded. Stockfish analysis started.");
  718:     return true;
  719:   }

  721:   function selectCandidate(candidate: Candidate) {
  722:     if (!session) return;
  723:     setCropEditingId(null);
  724:     cropBaselineRef.current = null;
  725:     patchSession({ selectedDiagramId: candidate.id });
  726:     setNotice("Region selected. The current chess position is unchanged; use Adjust Crop or Use on Board to continue.");
  727:   }
  728: 
  729:   function adjustSelectedCrop(candidate: Candidate) {
  730:     if (cropEditingId !== candidate.id) cropBaselineRef.current = candidate;
  731:     setCropEditingId(candidate.id);
  732:   }
  733: 
  734:   function resetSelectedCrop() {
  735:     const baseline = cropBaselineRef.current;
  736:     if (!baseline || !session) return;
  737:     patchSession((current) => {
  738:       const pageKey = String(current.page);
  739:       return {
  740:         ...current,
  741:         candidatesByPage: {
  742:           ...current.candidatesByPage,
  743:           [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
  744:             candidate.id === baseline.id ? baseline : candidate),
  745:         },
  746:       };
  747:     });
  748:   }
  749: 
  750:   function cancelCandidateSelection() {
  751:     const baseline = cropBaselineRef.current;
  752:     if (baseline && session) {
  753:       patchSession((current) => {
  754:         const pageKey = String(current.page);
  755:         return {
  756:           ...current,
  757:           candidatesByPage: {
  758:             ...current.candidatesByPage,
  759:             [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
  760:               candidate.id === baseline.id ? baseline : candidate),
  761:           },
  762:         };
  763:       });
  764:     }
  765:     setCropEditingId(null);
  766:     cropBaselineRef.current = null;
  767:     patchSession({ selectedDiagramId: null });
  768:   }
  769: 
  770:   async function openTransfer(candidate: Candidate) {
  771:     const canvas = canvasRef.current;
  772:     if (!canvas || canvas.width === 0 || canvas.height === 0) {
  773:       setError("The rendered page image is unavailable. Rerender the page before using this crop.");
  774:       return;
  775:     }
  776:     let preview: HTMLCanvasElement;
  777:     try {
  778:       const crop = cropToPixels(candidate, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  779:       preview = document.createElement("canvas");
  780:       preview.width = crop.width;
  781:       preview.height = crop.height;
  782:       const context = preview.getContext("2d");
  783:       if (!context) throw new Error("Could not prepare a crop preview in this browser.");
  784:       context.drawImage(canvas, crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height, 0, 0, crop.width, crop.height);
  785:     } catch (cause) {
  786:       setError(`Could not create the crop preview: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
  787:       return;
  788:     }
  789:     setTransferCandidate(candidate);
  790:     setTransferPreview(preview.toDataURL("image/png"));
  791:     setTransferFen("");
  792:     setRecognitionResult(null);
  793:         setRecognitionError("");
  794:         const requestId = ++transferRequestRef.current;
  795:     setRecognitionStatus("loading");
  796:     try {
  797:       const image = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
  798:         preview.toBlob((blob) => blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error("Could not encode the selected crop.")), "image/png");
  799:       });
  800:       const form = new FormData();
  801:       form.append("image", image, `board-crop-${candidate.id}.png`);
  802:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/recognize`, { method: "POST", body: form });
  803:       const result = await response.json() as RecognitionResult & { detail?: string };
  804:       if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.detail ?? `Recognition service returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
  805:       if (requestId !== transferRequestRef.current) return;
  806:       setTransferFen(result.fen);
  807:       setRecognitionResult(result);
  808:       setRecognitionStatus("complete");
  809:       // A successful scan immediately becomes the active analysis position.
  810:       // Keep the review dialog open so the user can correct uncertain squares;
  811:       // the explicit confirmation button remains available for corrected FENs.
  812:       if (moveToPosition(result.fen)) {
  813:         setNotice("Scanned position loaded automatically onto the analysis board. Review the diagram and correct any uncertain squares before continuing.");
  814:       }
  815:     } catch (cause) {
  816:       if (requestId !== transferRequestRef.current) return;
  817:       setRecognitionStatus("failed");
  818:       setRecognitionError(messageOf(cause));
  819:     }
  820:   }
  821: 
  822:   function closeTransfer() {
  823:     transferRequestRef.current += 1;
  824:     setTransferCandidate(null);
  825:     setTransferPreview("");
  826:     setTransferFen("");
  827:     setRecognitionStatus("idle");
  828:     setRecognitionResult(null);
  829:     setRecognitionError("");
  830:       }
  831: 
  832: 
  833:   function updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame: Chess) {
  834:     if (currentGame.isCheckmate()) {
  835:       setGameEndOverlay({
  836:         title: "Checkmate",
  837:         message: `${currentGame.turn() === "w" ? "Black" : "White"} wins.`,
  838:       });
  839:     } else if (currentGame.isStalemate()) {
  840:       setGameEndOverlay({
  841:         title: "Stalemate",
  842:         message: "The side to move has no legal moves, but its king is not in check. The game is a draw.",
  843:       });
  844:     } else if (currentGame.isDraw()) {
  845:       let message = "The game is drawn.";
  846:       if (currentGame.isThreefoldRepetition()) message = "Draw by threefold repetition.";
  847:       else if (currentGame.isInsufficientMaterial()) message = "Draw by insufficient material.";
  848:       else if (currentGame.isDrawByFiftyMoves()) message = "Draw by the fifty-move rule.";
  849:       else if (currentGame.isDraw()) message = "Draw by a draw rule.";
  850:       setGameEndOverlay({ title: "Draw", message });
  851:     } else {
  852:       setGameEndOverlay(null);
  853:     }
  854:   }
  855: 
  856:   function completePromotion(piece: "q" | "r" | "b" | "n") {
  857:     if (!promotionRequest || !boardSession) return;
  858:     const activeBoard = boardSession;
  859:     try {
  860:       const currentGame = new Chess(activeBoard.currentFen);
  861:       const result = currentGame.move({
  862:         from: promotionRequest.from,
  863:         to: promotionRequest.to,
  864:         promotion: piece,
  865:       });
  866:       const nextMoves = [...activeBoard.moves.slice(0, activeBoard.moveIndex), result.san];
  867:       patchBoardSession({
  868:         currentFen: currentGame.fen(),
  869:         moves: nextMoves,
  870:         moveIndex: nextMoves.length,
  871:       });
  872:       updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame);
  873:       setLastMove({ from: promotionRequest.from, to: promotionRequest.to });
  874:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  875:       setPromotionRequest(null);
  876:       setError("");
  877:     } catch {
  878:       setError("Unable to complete pawn promotion.");
  879:       setPromotionRequest(null);
  880:     }
  881:   }
  882: 
  883:   function makeMove(from: Square, to: Square) {
  884:     if (!boardSession) return;
  885:     const activeBoard = boardSession;
  886:     try {
  887:       const currentGame = new Chess(activeBoard.currentFen);
  888:       const mover = currentGame.get(from);
  889:       const promote = mover?.type === "p" && (to.endsWith("8") || to.endsWith("1"));
  890:       if (promote) {
  891:         setPromotionRequest({ from, to });
  892:         return;
  893:       }
  894:       const result = currentGame.move({ from, to });
  895:       const nextMoves = [...activeBoard.moves.slice(0, activeBoard.moveIndex), result.san];
  896:       if (workspaceMode === "opening-explorer") {
  897:         const line = nextMoves
  898:           .map((move, index) => index % 2 === 0 ? `${Math.floor(index / 2) + 1}. ${move}` : move)
  899:           .join(" ");
  900:         setOpeningExplorerInput(line);
  901:         setOpeningExplorerPly(nextMoves.length);
  902:       } else {
  903:         patchBoardSession({
  904:           currentFen: currentGame.fen(),
  905:           moves: nextMoves,
  906:           moveIndex: nextMoves.length,
  907:         });
  908:       }
  909:       updateGameEndOverlay(currentGame);
  910:       setLastMove({ from, to });
  911:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  912:       setError("");
  913:     } catch {
  914:       const piece = game.get(to);
  915:       if (piece && piece.color === game.turn()) setSelectedSquare(to);
  916:       else setSelectedSquare(null);
  917:     }
  918:   }
  919: 
  920:   function handleSquareClick(square: Square) {
  921:     // A left-click removes the red highlight from that square.
  922:     setRedSquares([]);
  923: 
  924:     if (!annotationMode && (userArrows.length > 0 || arrowStart !== null)) {
  925:       setUserArrows([]);
  926:       setArrowStart(null);
  927:       setArrowHover(null);
  928:       return;
  929:     }
  930: if (!boardSession) return;
  931:     if (annotationMode) {
  932:       if (!arrowStart) {
  933:         setArrowStart(square);
  934:         return;
  935:       }
  936:       if (arrowStart !== square) setUserArrows((arrows) => toggleArrow(arrows, { from: arrowStart, to: square }));
  937:       setArrowStart(null);
  938:       setArrowHover(null);
  939:       return;
  940:     }
  941:     if (selectedSquare && legalTargets.has(square)) {
  942:       makeMove(selectedSquare, square);
  943:       return;
  944:     }
  945:     const piece = game.get(square);
  946:     if (piece?.color === game.turn()) setSelectedSquare(square);
  947:     else setSelectedSquare(null);
  948:   }
  949: 
  950:   function boardArrowPointerDown(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  951:   if (event.button !== 2 || !boardRef.current) return;
  952: 
  953:   event.preventDefault();
  954: 
  955:   const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  956:   const square = boardSquareAtPoint(
  957:     event.clientX,
  958:     event.clientY,
  959:     bounds,
  960:     Boolean(boardSession?.flipped),
  961:   );
  962: 
  963:   if (!square) return;
  964: 
  965:   // Only suppress the context menu if an arrow is actually drawn.
  966:   suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
  967:   arrowGestureMovedRef.current = false;
  968:   arrowGestureRef.current = square;
  969:   setArrowStart(square);
  970:   setArrowHover(square);
  971: 
  972:   event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
  973: }
  974: 
  975:   function boardArrowPointerMove(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  976:     if (!arrowGestureRef.current || !boardRef.current) return;
  977:     const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  978:     const square = boardSquareAtPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY, bounds, Boolean(boardSession?.flipped));
  979:     if (square && square !== arrowGestureRef.current) arrowGestureMovedRef.current = true;
  980:     setArrowHover(square);
  981:   }
  982: 
  983:   function boardArrowPointerUp(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
  984:   const from = arrowGestureRef.current;
  985: 
  986:   if (!from || !boardRef.current) return;
  987: 
  988:   const bounds = boardRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
  989:   const to = boardSquareAtPoint(
  990:     event.clientX,
  991:     event.clientY,
  992:     bounds,
  993:     Boolean(boardSession?.flipped),
  994:   );
  995: 
  996:   if (to && to !== from) {
  997:     setUserArrows((arrows) => toggleArrow(arrows, { from, to }));
  998:     suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = true;
  999:   } else {
 1000:     suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
 1001:   }
 1002: 
 1003:   arrowGestureRef.current = null;
 1004:   arrowGestureMovedRef.current = false;
 1005:   setArrowStart(null);
 1006:   setArrowHover(null);
 1007: }
 1008: 
 1009:   function cancelBoardArrowGesture() {
 1010:     arrowGestureRef.current = null;
 1011:     setArrowStart(null);
 1012:     setArrowHover(null);
 1013:   }
 1014: 
 1015:   function loadPgnText(source: string) {
 1016:     try {
 1017:       const imported = parsePgn(source);
 1018:       const replay = new Chess(imported.lineBaseFen);
 1019: 
 1020:       for (const san of imported.moves) {
 1021:         replay.move(san);
 1022:       }
 1023: 
 1024:       const last = replay.history({ verbose: true }).at(-1);
 1025: 
 1026:       setAnalysisBoard((current) => ({
 1027:         ...current,
 1028:         lineBaseFen: imported.lineBaseFen,
 1029:         currentFen: imported.finalFen,
 1030:         moves: imported.moves,
 1031:         moveIndex: imported.moves.length,
 1032:       }));
 1033: 
 1034:       setWorkspaceMode("analysis");
 1035:       setStudyMode(false);
 1036:       setLastMove(last ? { from: last.from, to: last.to } : null);
 1037:       setSelectedSquare(null);
 1038: 
 1039:       const white = imported.headers.White || "White";
 1040:       const black = imported.headers.Black || "Black";
 1041:       setNotice(`PGN loaded: ${white} vs ${black} · ${imported.moves.length} plies.`);
 1042:       setError("");
 1043:     } catch (cause) {
 1044:       setError(`PGN import failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1045:     }
 1046:   }
 1047: 
 1048:   async function importPgnFile(file: File) {
 1049:     try {
 1050:       const source = await file.text();
 1051:       loadPgnText(source);
 1052:     } catch (cause) {
 1053:       setError(`Could not read PGN file: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1054:     }
 1055:   }
 1056: 
 1057:   function pastePgn() {
 1058:     const source = window.prompt("Paste PGN notation:");
 1059:     if (source !== null) loadPgnText(source);
 1060:   }
 1061:   function jumpToMove(index: number) {
 1062:     if (!boardSession) return;
 1063:     try {
 1064:       const next = makePosition(boardSession.lineBaseFen, boardSession.moves, index);
 1065:       const last = next.history({ verbose: true }).at(-1);
 1066:       patchBoardSession({ moveIndex: index, currentFen: next.fen() });
 1067:       setLastMove(last ? { from: last.from, to: last.to } : null);
 1068:       setSelectedSquare(null);
 1069:     } catch (cause) {
 1070:       setError(`Could not navigate to move: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1071:     }
 1072:   }
 1073: 
 1074:   async function copyText(value: string) {
 1075:     try {
 1076:       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
 1077:       setNotice("Copied to clipboard.");
 1078:     } catch (cause) {
 1079:       setError(`Clipboard access failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1080:     }
 1081:   }
 1082: 
 1083:   function stopBoardScan() {
 1084:     const controller = scanAbortControllerRef.current;
 1085:     if (!controller) return;
 1086: 
 1087:     ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1088:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1089:     controller.abort();
 1090:     setDetecting(false);
 1091:     setNotice("Scan cancelled.");
 1092: 
 1093:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1094:       ...current,
 1095:       candidatesByPage: {
 1096:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1097:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1098:       },
 1099:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1100:     }));
 1101:   }
 1102: 
 1103: 
 1104:   function clearBoardScan() {
 1105:     // Invalidate any pending response before aborting the request.
 1106:     ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1107: 
 1108:     const controller = scanAbortControllerRef.current;
 1109:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1110:     controller?.abort();
 1111: 
 1112:     setDetecting(false);
 1113:     setManualMode(false);
 1114:     setDragSelection(null);
 1115:     setCropEditingId(null);
 1116:     cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1117:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1118: 
 1119:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1120:       ...current,
 1121:       candidatesByPage: {
 1122:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1123:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1124:       },
 1125:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1126:     }));
 1127: 
 1128:     setError("");
 1129:     setNotice("Board detection cleared.");
 1130:   }
 1131:   async function detectBoards() {
 1132:     if (detecting) {
 1133:       stopBoardScan();
 1134:       return;
 1135:     }
 1136: 
 1137:     if (!canvasRef.current || !session) return;
 1138:     if (!API_BASE) {
 1139:       setError("Board detection API is not configured. Configure VITE_API_BASE_URL and start the optional CV service.");
 1140:       return;
 1141:     }
 1142: 
 1143:     scanAbortControllerRef.current?.abort();
 1144:     const abortController = new AbortController();
 1145:     scanAbortControllerRef.current = abortController;
 1146:     const requestId = ++scanRequestIdRef.current;
 1147: 
 1148:     setDetecting(true);
 1149:     setError("");
 1150:     setNotice("Scanning current page...");
 1151:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1152:       ...current,
 1153:       candidatesByPage: {
 1154:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1155:         [String(current.page)]: [],
 1156:       },
 1157:       selectedDiagramId: null,
 1158:     }));
 1159: 
 1160:     try {
 1161:       const image = await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
 1162:         canvasRef.current?.toBlob(
 1163:           (blob) => blob
 1164:             ? resolve(blob)
 1165:             : reject(new Error("Could not encode rendered PDF page.")),
 1166:           "image/png",
 1167:         );
 1168:       });
 1169: 
 1170:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1171: 
 1172:       const form = new FormData();
 1173:       form.append("image", image, `page-${session.page}.png`);
 1174: 
 1175:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/detect`, {
 1176:         method: "POST",
 1177:         body: form,
 1178:         signal: abortController.signal,
 1179:       });
 1180: 
 1181:       if (!response.ok) {
 1182:         throw new Error(`Detector returned HTTP ${response.status}.`);
 1183:       }
 1184: 
 1185:       const data = await response.json() as { candidates?: CandidateInput[] };
 1186:       const candidates = (data.candidates ?? []).map(
 1187:         (item, index) => normalizedCandidate({ ...item, source: "automatic" }, index),
 1188:       );
 1189: 
 1190:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1191: 
 1192:       patchSession((current) => ({
 1193:         ...current,
 1194:         candidatesByPage: {
 1195:           ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1196:           [String(current.page)]: candidates,
 1197:         },
 1198:         selectedDiagramId: null,
 1199:       }));
 1200: 
 1201:       setNotice(
 1202:         candidates.length
 1203:           ? `Found ${candidates.length} board candidate${candidates.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}. Select a candidate to recognize its chess position and load it onto the analysis board.`
 1204:           : "No board candidates found.",
 1205:       );
 1206:     } catch (cause) {
 1207:       if (requestId !== scanRequestIdRef.current) return;
 1208: 
 1209:       if (abortController.signal.aborted) {
 1210:         setNotice("Scan cancelled.");
 1211:       } else {
 1212:         setError(`Board detection failed: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1213:       }
 1214:     } finally {
 1215:       if (requestId === scanRequestIdRef.current) {
 1216:         scanAbortControllerRef.current = null;
 1217:         setDetecting(false);
 1218:       }
 1219:     }
 1220:   }
 1221:   function pointerPosition(event: { clientX: number; clientY: number }) {
 1222:     const box = canvasRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();
 1223:     return box ? pointOnCanvas(event.clientX, event.clientY, box) : null;
 1224:   }
 1225: 
 1226:   function startCropEdit(
 1227:     event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLElement>,
 1228:     candidate: Candidate,
 1229:     handle: CropHandle,
 1230:   ) {
 1231:     event.stopPropagation();
 1232:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1233:     const stage = pageStageRef.current;
 1234:     if (!point || !stage) return;
 1235:     cropEditStartRef.current = { candidate, handle, point };
 1236:     stage.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
 1237:   }
 1238: 
 1239:   function startRegion(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
 1240:     if (cropEditStartRef.current) return;
 1241:     if (!manualMode) return;
 1242: const canvas = canvasRef.current;
 1243: if (!canvas) return;
 1244: const target = event.target as HTMLElement;
 1245: if (target.closest(".diagram-overlay, .crop-handle")) return;
 1246: const bounds = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
 1247: if (
 1248:   event.clientX < bounds.left ||
 1249:   event.clientX > bounds.right ||
 1250:   event.clientY < bounds.top ||
 1251:   event.clientY > bounds.bottom
 1252: ) return;
 1253:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1254:     if (!point) return;
 1255:     dragStartRef.current = point;
 1256:     const selection = { ...point, x2: point.x, y2: point.y };
 1257:     dragCurrentRef.current = selection;
 1258:     setDragSelection(selection);
 1259:     event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
 1260:   }
 1261: 
 1262:   function moveRegion(event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
 1263:     const edit = cropEditStartRef.current;
 1264:     if (edit) {
 1265:       const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1266:       if (!point || !session) return;
 1267:       const next = editCrop(edit.candidate, edit.handle, point.x - edit.point.x, point.y - edit.point.y);
 1268:       patchSession((current) => {
 1269:         const pageKey = String(current.page);
 1270:         return {
 1271:           ...current,
 1272:           candidatesByPage: {
 1273:             ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1274:             [pageKey]: (current.candidatesByPage[pageKey] ?? []).map((candidate) =>
 1275:               candidate.id === edit.candidate.id ? { ...candidate, ...next } : candidate),
 1276:           },
 1277:         };
 1278:       });
 1279:       return;
 1280:     }
 1281:     if (!dragStartRef.current) return;
 1282:     const point = pointerPosition(event);
 1283:     if (point) {
 1284:       const selection = { ...dragStartRef.current, x2: point.x, y2: point.y };
 1285:       dragCurrentRef.current = selection;
 1286:       setDragSelection(selection);
 1287:     }
 1288:   }
 1289: 
 1290:   function finishRegion() {
 1291:     if (cropEditStartRef.current) {
 1292:       cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1293:       return;
 1294:     }
 1295:     const selection = dragCurrentRef.current;
 1296:     if (!dragStartRef.current || !selection || !session) return;
 1297:     const x = Math.min(selection.x, selection.x2);
 1298:     const y = Math.min(selection.y, selection.y2);
 1299:     const width = Math.abs(selection.x2 - selection.x);
 1300:     const height = Math.abs(selection.y2 - selection.y);
 1301:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1302:     dragCurrentRef.current = null;
 1303:     setDragSelection(null);
 1304:     if (width < 0.025 || height < 0.025) return;
 1305:     const candidate: Candidate = {
 1306:       id: `manual-${crypto.randomUUID()}`,
 1307:       x, y, width, height,
 1308:       relativeScore: 1,
 1309:       source: "manual",
 1310:     };
 1311:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1312:       ...current,
 1313:       candidatesByPage: {
 1314:         ...current.candidatesByPage,
 1315:         [String(current.page)]: [...(current.candidatesByPage[String(current.page)] ?? []), candidate],
 1316:       },
 1317:       selectedDiagramId: candidate.id,
 1318:     }));
 1319:     setManualMode(false);
 1320:     setNotice("Manual crop selected. Adjust its boundary, then use it on the board when ready.");
 1321:   }
 1322: 
 1323:   function cancelRegion() {
 1324:     cropEditStartRef.current = null;
 1325:     dragStartRef.current = null;
 1326:     dragCurrentRef.current = null;
 1327:     setDragSelection(null);
 1328:   }
 1329: 
 1330:   async function fitPage(mode: "width" | "page") {
 1331:     if (!pdf || !session || !pageStageRef.current) return;
 1332:     try {
 1333:       const page = await pdf.getPage(session.page);
 1334:       const base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
 1335:       const renderScale = Math.min(1.4, 1100 / base.width);
 1336:       const availableWidth = Math.max(200, pageStageRef.current.parentElement?.clientWidth ?? base.width) - 44;
 1337:       const availableHeight = Math.max(250, pageStageRef.current.parentElement?.clientHeight ?? base.height) - 52;
 1338:       const scale = mode === "width"
 1339:         ? availableWidth / (base.width * renderScale)
 1340:         : Math.min(availableWidth / base.width, availableHeight / base.height) / renderScale;
 1341:       patchSession({ zoom: Math.max(0.45, Math.min(1.8, scale)) });
 1342:     } catch (cause) {
 1343:       setError(`Could not fit PDF page: ${messageOf(cause)}`);
 1344:     }
 1345:   }
 1346:

 1348: 
 1349:   function saveBookmark() {
 1350:     if (!session) return;
 1351:     patchSession((current) => ({
 1352:       ...current,
 1353:       bookmarks: current.bookmarks.includes(current.page)
 1354:         ? current.bookmarks.filter((page) => page !== current.page)
 1355:         : [...current.bookmarks, current.page].sort((a, b) => a - b),
 1356:     }));
 1357:   }

 1359:   function savePosition() {

 1361:     const position = {
 1362:       id: crypto.randomUUID(),
 1363:       title: `Page ${session.page} position`,
 1364:       fen: session.currentFen,
 1365:       page: session.page,
 1366:       savedAt: Date.now(),
 1367:     };
 1368:     patchSession((current) => ({ ...current, positions: [...current.positions, position] }));
 1369:     setNotice("Position saved to this book.");
 1370:   }

 1521:

 1523:     suppressBoardContextMenuRef.current = false;
 1524:     return;

 1529:

 1537:

 1539:     current.includes(square)
 1540:       ? current.filter((item) => item !== square)
 1541:       : [...current, square]
 1542:   );
 1543: }}

 1549:                   const rankIndex = boardSession?.flipped ? visibleRow : 7 - visibleRow;
 1550:                   const square = squareName(fileIndex, rankIndex);
 1551:                   const piece = pieceSquares[7 - rankIndex]?.[fileIndex];
 1552:                   const legal = legalTargets.has(square);
 1553:                   const isLast = highlightedMove?.from === square || highlightedMove?.to === square;
 1554:                   const light = (fileIndex + rankIndex) % 2 === 1;
 1555:                   return (
 1556:                     <button
 1557:                       key={square}
 1558:                       className={`square ${light ? "light-square" : "dark-square"} ${selectedSquare === square ? "selected-square" : ""} ${isLast ? "last-square" : ""} ${redSquares.includes(square) ? "red-highlight" : ""}`}
 1559:                       aria-label={`${square}${piece ? ` ${piece.color === "w" ? "white" : "black"} ${piece.type}` : ""}`}
 1560:                       draggable={Boolean(piece && piece.color === game.turn())}
 1561:                       onClick={() => handleSquareClick(square)}
 1562:                       onDragStart={(event) => {
 1563:                         const image = event.currentTarget.querySelector("img");
 1564:                         if (image) {
 1565:                           const bounds = event.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
 1566:                           event.dataTransfer.setDragImage(image, bounds.width / 2, bounds.height / 2);
 1567:                         }
 1568:                         event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", square);
 1569:                         setSelectedSquare(square);
 1570:                       }}
 1571:                       onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
 1572:                       onDrop={(event) => { event.preventDefault(); const from = event.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain") as Square; if (from) makeMove(from, square); }}
 1573:                       onDragEnd={() => setSelectedSquare(null)}
 1574:                     >
 1575:                       {piece && <span className={`piece ${piece.color === "w" ? "white-piece" : "black-piece"}`} aria-hidden="true"><img draggable={false} alt="" src={`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standalone/${piece.color}${piece.type}.svg`} /></span>}
 1576:                       {legal && <span className={piece ? "legal-capture" : "legal-dot"} />}
 1577:                       {boardSession?.coordinates && visibleCol === 0 && <span className={`rank-coordinate ${light ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>{rankIndex + 1}</span>}
 1578:                       {boardSession?.coordinates && visibleRow === 7 && <span className={`file-coordinate ${light ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>{"abcdefgh"[fileIndex]}</span>}
 1579:                     </button>
 1580:                   );
 1581:                 })}
 1582:                 <svg className="board-arrows" viewBox="0 0 8 8" aria-hidden="true">
 1583:                   {[
 1584:                     ...userArrows.map((arrow) => ({ arrow, kind: "user" as const })),
 1585:                     ...(engineArrow ? [{ arrow: engineArrow, kind: "engine" as const }] : []),
 1586:                     ...(arrowStart && arrowHover && arrowStart !== arrowHover
 1587:                       ? [{ arrow: { from: arrowStart, to: arrowHover }, kind: "preview" as const }]
 1588:                       : []),
 1589:                   ].map(({ arrow, kind }, index) => {
 1590:                     const from = squareCenter(arrow.from, Boolean(boardSession?.flipped));
 1591:                     const to = squareCenter(arrow.to, Boolean(boardSession?.flipped));
 1592:                     const dx = to.x - from.x;
 1593:                     const dy = to.y - from.y;
 1594:                     const length = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
 1595:                     if (length < 0.001) return null;
 1596: 
 1597:                     const ux = dx / length;
 1598:                     const uy = dy / length;
 1599:                     const px = -uy;
 1600:                     const py = ux;
 1601: 
 1602:                     const headLength = Math.min(0.34, length * 0.30);
 1603:                     const headHalfWidth = 0.19;
 1604:                     const shaftHalfWidth = kind === "preview" ? 0.045 : 0.055;
 1605:                     const tipX = to.x - ux * 0.04;
 1606:                     const tipY = to.y - uy * 0.04;
 1607:                     const baseX = tipX - ux * headLength;
 1608:                     const baseY = tipY - uy * headLength;
 1609:                     const startX = from.x + ux * 0.12;
 1610:                     const startY = from.y + uy * 0.12;
 1611: 
 1612:                     const points = [
 1613:                       [startX + px * shaftHalfWidth, startY + py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1614:                       [baseX + px * shaftHalfWidth, baseY + py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1615:                       [baseX + px * headHalfWidth, baseY + py * headHalfWidth],
 1616:                       [tipX, tipY],
 1617:                       [baseX - px * headHalfWidth, baseY - py * headHalfWidth],
 1618:                       [baseX - px * shaftHalfWidth, baseY - py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1619:                       [startX - px * shaftHalfWidth, startY - py * shaftHalfWidth],
 1620:                     ];
 1621:                     const color = kind === "engine" ? "#f0a23b" : "#81b64c";
 1622:                     const opacity = kind === "preview" ? 0.52 : kind === "engine" ? 0.78 : 0.80;
 1623: 
 1624:                     return (
 1625:                       <polygon
 1626:                         key={`${kind}-${arrow.from}-${arrow.to}-${index}`}
 1627:                         points={points.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`).join(" ")}
 1628:                         fill={color}
 1629:                         opacity={opacity}
 1630:                         className={`board-arrow board-arrow-${kind}`}
 1631:                       />
 1632:                     );
 1633:                   })}
 1634:                 </svg>
 1635:               </div>
 1636:             </div>
 1637:           </div>

 1947:             </div>}
 1948:             <div className="scan-hint">{currentDiagram ? "Selection does not change the board; confirm with Use on Board." : "Select a detected board or draw a region."}</div>
 1949:           </div>
 1950:           <div className="analysis-strip">
 1951:             <div className="analysis-name"><span className="stockfish-mark"><Cpu size={19} strokeWidth={2} aria-hidden="true" /></span><div><strong>Stockfish 19 Lite</strong><span>{engineStatus === "error" ? "Engine unavailable" : engineStatus === "off" ? "Engine off" : engineStatus === "loading" ? "Loading engineÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : engineStatus === "analyzing" ? "Analyzing current positionÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Ready Ã‚Â· White-positive scores"}</span></div></div>
 1952:             <div className="analysis-divider" />
 1953:             <button className={`engine-toggle ${engineEnabled ? "engine-active" : ""}`} onClick={toggleEngine}>{engineEnabled ? "Stop analysis" : engineStatus === "error" ? "Retry Stockfish" : "Analyze position"}</button>
 1954:             <label className="depth-control">Depth <select value={engineDepth} onChange={(event) => setEngineDepth(Number(event.target.value))}><option value={10}>10</option><option value={12}>12</option><option value={14}>14</option><option value={16}>16</option><option value={18}>18</option></select></label>
 1955:             <div className="analysis-result"><span>Evaluation Ã‚Â· White</span><strong>{engineScore ? formatEvaluation(engineScore) : "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</strong></div>
 1956:             <div className="analysis-result best-result"><span>Best move</span><strong>{engineScore?.bestMove ? formatUciMove(game.fen(), engineScore.bestMove) : "Ã¢â‚¬â€"}</strong></div>
 1957:             <div className="analysis-pv"><span>PRINCIPAL VARIATION</span><strong>{engineStatus === "error" ? engineMessage : engineScore?.pv ? formatUciPv(game.fen(), engineScore.pv) : (engineEnabled ? "Waiting for first valid scoreÃ¢â‚¬Â¦" : "Enable Stockfish to analyze this position.")}</strong></div>
 1958:           </div>
 1959:         </section>
 1960:         </>}
 1961:       </div>
 1962: 
 1963:       {settingsOpen && <div className="settings-popover"><strong>Workspace settings</strong><span>Books and analysis sessions are stored in IndexedDB on this device.</span><span>Stockfish runs locally in a browser worker. Cloud sync is not configured.</span><button onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(false)}>Close</button></div>}
 1964:       {transferCandidate && (
 1965:         <ManualBoardEditor
 1966:           initialFen={transferFen || (recognitionResult?.fen ?? START_FEN)}
 1967:           title="Review recognized position"
 1968:           subtitle="The scan is already loaded on the analysis board. Review and correct any uncertain squares, then confirm your edits."
 1969:           confirmLabel="Use on Board"
 1970:           cropPreviewUrl={transferPreview}
 1971:           recognitionStatus={recognitionStatus}
 1972:           recognitionResult={recognitionResult}
 1973:           recognitionError={recognitionError}
 1974:           onRetryRecognition={() => void openTransfer(transferCandidate)}
 1975:           onConfirm={(confirmedFen) => {
 1976:             if (moveToPosition(confirmedFen)) {
 1977:               closeTransfer();
 1978:             }
 1979:           }}
 1980:           onCancel={closeTransfer}
 1981:         />
 1982:       )}
 1983:       {setupOpen && (
 1984:         <ManualBoardEditor
 1985:           initialFen={game.fen()}
 1986:           title="Set Up Position"
 1987:           subtitle="Set up a custom chess position to analyze with Stockfish."
 1988:           confirmLabel="Analyze Position"
 1989:           showPresets={true}
 1990:           currentAnalysisFen={game.fen()}
 1991:           onConfirm={(confirmedFen) => {
 1992:             if (analyzeManualPosition(confirmedFen)) {
 1993:               setSetupOpen(false);
 1994:             }
 1995:           }}
 1996:           onCancel={() => setSetupOpen(false)}
 1997:         />
 1998:       )}
 1999:       {gameEndOverlay && (
 2000:         <div className="game-end-backdrop">
 2001:           <section className="game-end-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="game-end-title">
 2002:             <div className="game-end-icon" aria-hidden="true">
 2003:               {gameEndOverlay.title === "Checkmate" ? "Ã¢â„¢â€" : gameEndOverlay.title === "Stalemate" ? "Ã‚Â½" : "Ã‚Â½"}
 2004:             </div>
 2005:             <h2 id="game-end-title">{gameEndOverlay.title}</h2>
 2006:             <p>{gameEndOverlay.message}</p>
 2007:             <div className="game-end-actions">
 2008:               <button type="button" onClick={() => setGameEndOverlay(null)}>Review position</button>
 2009:               <button type="button" onClick={() => { setGameEndOverlay(null); setSelectedSquare(null); }}>Continue analysis</button>
 2010:             </div>
 2011:           </section>
 2012:         </div>
 2013:       )}
 2014:       {promotionRequest && (
 2015:         <div className="promotion-backdrop" onClick={() => setPromotionRequest(null)}>
 2016:           <section className="promotion-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="promotion-title" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}>
 2017:             <h2 id="promotion-title">Promote pawn</h2>
 2018:             <p>Choose a piece to complete your move.</p>
 2019:             <div className="promotion-choices">
 2020:               <button type="button" onClick={() => completePromotion("q")} aria-label="Promote to queen"><span>Ã¢â„¢â€¢</span><small>Queen</small></button>
 2021:               <button type="button" onClick={() => completePromotion("r")} aria-label="Promote to rook"><span>Ã¢â„¢â€“</span><small>Rook</small></button>
 2022:               <button type="button" onClick={() => completePromotion("b")} aria-label="Promote to bishop"><span>Ã¢â„¢â€”</span><small>Bishop</small></button>
 2023:               <button type="button" onClick={() => completePromotion("n")} aria-label="Promote to knight"><span>Ã¢â„¢Ëœ</span><small>Knight</small></button>
 2024:             </div>
 2025:             <button className="promotion-cancel" type="button" onClick={() => setPromotionRequest(null)}>Cancel</button>
 2026:           </section>
 2027:         </div>
 2028:       )}
 2029:             {(error || notice) && <div className={`toast ${error ? "toast-error" : ""}`} role={error ? "alert" : "status"}><span>{error || notice}</span><button aria-label="Dismiss message" onClick={() => { setError(""); setNotice(""); }}>&#215;</button></div>}
 2030:     </main>
 2031:   );
 2032: }
 2033: 
 2034: function LibraryNavItem({
 2035:   active,
 2036:   onClick,
 2037:   icon: Icon,
 2038:   label,
 2039: }: {
 2040:   active: boolean;
 2041:   onClick: () => void;
 2042:   icon: React.ComponentType<{ size?: number; strokeWidth?: number; "aria-hidden"?: boolean }>;
 2043:   label: string;
 2044: }) {
 2045:   return (
 2046:     <button
 2047:       type="button"
 2048:       className={`library-nav-item ${active ? "nav-active" : ""}`}
 2049:       onClick={onClick}
 2050:       aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
 2051:     >
 2052:       <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={1.8} aria-hidden={true} />
 2053:       <span>{label}</span>
 2054:     </button>
 2055:   );
 2056: }
 2057: 
 2058: function formatSize(bytes: number): string {
 2059:   if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`;
 2060:   return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`;
 2061: }
 2062: 
 2063: function isTypingTarget(target: EventTarget | null): boolean {
 2064:   return target instanceof HTMLElement && (target.isContentEditable || ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA", "SELECT"].includes(target.tagName));
 2065: }

========== src\model.ts ==========
    1: export const START_FEN = "rnbqkbnr/pppppppp/8/8/8/8/PPPPPPPP/RNBQKBNR w KQkq - 0 1";
    2: 
    3: export type Candidate = {
    4:   id: string;
    5:   x: number;
    6:   y: number;
    7:   width: number;
    8:   height: number;
    9:   relativeScore?: number;
   10:   confidence?: number;
   11:   source: "automatic" | "manual";
   12: };
   13: 
   14: export type BookRecord = {
   15:   id: string;
   16:   title: string;
   17:   file: Blob;
   18:   byteLength: number;
   19:   lastOpenedAt: number;
   20:   addedAt: number;
   21: };
   22: 
   23: export type SavedPosition = {
   24:   id: string;
   25:   title: string;
   26:   fen: string;
   27:   page: number;
   28:   savedAt: number;
   29: };
   30: 
   31: export type BookSession = {
   32:   bookId: string;
   33:   page: number;
   34:   pageCount: number;
   35:   zoom: number;
   36:   selectedDiagramId: string | null;
   37:   candidatesByPage: Record<string, Candidate[]>;
   38:   originalFen: string;
   39:   lineBaseFen: string;
   40:   currentFen: string;
   41:   moves: string[];
   42:   moveIndex: number;
   43:   flipped: boolean;
   44:   coordinates: boolean;
   45:   bookmarks: number[];
   46:   positions: SavedPosition[];
   47: };
   48: 
   49: export function newSession(bookId: string): BookSession {
   50:   return {
   51:     bookId,
   52:     page: 1,
   53:     pageCount: 0,
   54:     zoom: 1,
   55:     selectedDiagramId: null,
   56:     candidatesByPage: {},
   57:     originalFen: START_FEN,
   58:     lineBaseFen: START_FEN,
   59:     currentFen: START_FEN,
   60:     moves: [],
   61:     moveIndex: 0,
   62:     flipped: false,
   63:     coordinates: true,
   64:     bookmarks: [],
   65:     positions: [],
   66:   };
   67: }

========== src\chess-state.ts ==========
    1: import { Chess } from "chess.js";
    2: 
    3: export function makePosition(baseFen: string, moves: string[], moveIndex: number): Chess {
    4:   const game = new Chess(baseFen);
    5:   for (const san of moves.slice(0, moveIndex)) {
    6:     const result = game.move(san);
    7:     if (!result) throw new Error(`Saved move could not be replayed: ${san}`);
    8:   }
    9:   return game;
   10: }
   11: 
   12: export function fenWithTurn(fen: string, turn: "w" | "b"): string {
   13:   const fields = fen.split(/\s+/);
   14:   if (fields.length !== 6) throw new Error("FEN must contain six fields.");
   15:   fields[1] = turn;
   16:   fields[3] = "-";
   17:   fields[4] = "0";
   18:   return fields.join(" ");
   19: }

========== src\pgn-import.ts ==========
    1: import { Chess } from "chess.js";
    2: import { START_FEN } from "./model";
    3: 
    4: export type ImportedPgn = {
    5:   headers: Record<string, string>;
    6:   lineBaseFen: string;
    7:   moves: string[];
    8:   finalFen: string;
    9: };
   10: 
   11: export function parsePgn(pgn: string): ImportedPgn {
   12:   const source = pgn.trim();
   13: 
   14:   if (!source) {
   15:     throw new Error("Paste or select a PGN game first.");
   16:   }
   17: 
   18:   const parsed = new Chess();
   19: 
   20:   try {
   21:     parsed.loadPgn(source);
   22:   } catch (cause) {
   23:     const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Unknown PGN error";
   24:     throw new Error(`Invalid PGN: ${detail}`);
   25:   }
   26: 
   27:   const headers = parsed.getHeaders();
   28:   const customFen = headers.FEN?.trim();
   29:   const lineBaseFen = customFen || START_FEN;
   30: 
   31:   let replay: Chess;
   32: 
   33:   try {
   34:     replay = new Chess(lineBaseFen);
   35:   } catch {
   36:     throw new Error("The PGN contains an invalid starting FEN.");
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   const moves = parsed.history();
   40: 
   41:   if (moves.length === 0) {
   42:     throw new Error("The PGN contains no moves.");
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   try {
   46:     for (const san of moves) {
   47:       replay.move(san);
   48:     }
   49:   } catch (cause) {
   50:     const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : "Unknown move error";
   51:     throw new Error(`Could not replay the imported game: ${detail}`);
   52:   }
   53: 
   54:   if (replay.fen() !== parsed.fen()) {
   55:     throw new Error("The imported PGN could not be reproduced from its starting position.");
   56:   }
   57: 
   58:   return {
   59:     headers,
   60:     lineBaseFen,
   61:     moves,
   62:     finalFen: replay.fen(),
   63:   };
   64: }

========== src\ManualBoardEditor.tsx ==========
    1: import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    2: import {
    3:   buildFullFen,
    4:   countKings,
    5:   EMPTY_PLACEMENT,
    6:   flipFenPlacement,
    7:   moveFenPiece,
    8:   parseFenPlacement,
    9:   PIECE_LABELS,
   10:   sanitizeCastlingRights,
   11:   setFenPiece,
   12:   START_PLACEMENT,
   13:   validatePositionFen,
   14: } from "./position-editor";
   15: 
   16: const WHITE_PIECES = ["K", "Q", "R", "B", "N", "P"] as const;
   17: const BLACK_PIECES = ["k", "q", "r", "b", "n", "p"] as const;
   18: 
   19: export interface ManualBoardEditorProps {
   20:   initialFen: string;
   21:   onConfirm: (fen: string) => void;
   22:   onCancel: () => void;
   23:   confirmLabel: string;
   24:   title: string;
   25:   subtitle?: string;
   26:   // Specific to recognition review (Feature A)
   27:   cropPreviewUrl?: string;
   28:   recognitionStatus?: "idle" | "loading" | "complete" | "failed";
   29:   recognitionResult?: {
   30:     board_confidence: number;
   31:     mean_piece_confidence: number | null;
   32:     piece_count: number;
   33:     orientation: "normal" | "flipped" | "unknown";
   34:     warnings: string[];
   35:   } | null;
   36:   recognitionError?: string;
   37:   onRetryRecognition?: () => void;
   38:   // Specific to setup mode (Feature B)
   39:   showPresets?: boolean;
   40:   currentAnalysisFen?: string;
   41: }
   42: 
   43: export function ManualBoardEditor({
   44:   initialFen,
   45:   onConfirm,
   46:   onCancel,
   47:   confirmLabel,
   48:   title,
   49:   subtitle,
   50:   cropPreviewUrl,
   51:   recognitionStatus,
   52:   recognitionResult,
   53:   recognitionError,
   54:   onRetryRecognition,
   55:   showPresets = false,
   56:   currentAnalysisFen,
   57: }: ManualBoardEditorProps) {
   58:   // Parse initial fields
   59:   const parsedInitial = useMemo(() => {
   60:     const trimmed = initialFen.trim();
   61:     const fields = trimmed ? trimmed.split(/\s+/) : [];
   62:     const placement = fields[0] && parseFenPlacement(fields[0]) ? fields[0] : START_PLACEMENT;
   63:     const turn = fields[1] === "b" ? ("b" as const) : ("w" as const);
   64:     const castling = fields[2] ?? "-";
   65:     const ep = fields[3] ?? "-";
   66:     const halfmove = Number(fields[4]) || 0;
   67:     const fullmove = Number(fields[5]) || 1;
   68:     const fen = buildFullFen(placement, turn, castling, ep, halfmove, fullmove);
   69:     return { placement, turn, castling, ep, halfmove, fullmove, fen };
   70:   }, [initialFen]);
   71: 
   72:   const [placement, setPlacement] = useState(parsedInitial.placement);
   73:   const [turn, setTurn] = useState<"w" | "b">(parsedInitial.turn);
   74:   const [castling, setCastling] = useState(parsedInitial.castling);
   75:   const [activeTool, setActiveTool] = useState<string>("P"); // Piece symbol or "" for erase
   76:   const [selectedSquare, setSelectedSquare] = useState<{ row: number; col: number } | null>(null);
   77:   const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false);
   78:   const [history, setHistory] = useState<string[]>([parsedInitial.fen]);
   79:   const [historyIndex, setHistoryIndex] = useState(0);
   80:   const [fenInputText, setFenInputText] = useState(parsedInitial.fen);
   81:   const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState<string>("");
   82: 
   83:   // When initialFen changes externally (e.g. recognition completes or retries), reset editor
   84:   useEffect(() => {
   85:     setPlacement(parsedInitial.placement);
   86:     setTurn(parsedInitial.turn);
   87:     setCastling(parsedInitial.castling);
   88:     setHistory([parsedInitial.fen]);
   89:     setHistoryIndex(0);
   90:     setFenInputText(parsedInitial.fen);
   91:     setValidationError("");
   92:     setSelectedSquare(null);
   93:   }, [parsedInitial]);
   94: 
   95:   const currentBoard = useMemo(() => parseFenPlacement(placement) ?? parseFenPlacement(EMPTY_PLACEMENT)!, [placement]);
   96:   const currentFen = useMemo(() => buildFullFen(placement, turn, castling), [placement, turn, castling]);
   97:   const validation = useMemo(() => validatePositionFen(currentFen), [currentFen]);
   98:   const kingCounts = useMemo(() => countKings(placement), [placement]);
   99: 
  100:   // Push new state to history
  101:   function pushState(newPlacement: string, newTurn: "w" | "b" = turn, newCastling: string = castling) {
  102:     const board = parseFenPlacement(newPlacement);
  103:     const sanitizedCastling = board ? sanitizeCastlingRights(board, newCastling) : "-";
  104:     const newFen = buildFullFen(newPlacement, newTurn, sanitizedCastling);
  105: 
  106:     setPlacement(newPlacement);
  107:     setTurn(newTurn);
  108:     setCastling(sanitizedCastling);
  109:     setFenInputText(newFen);
  110:     setValidationError("");
  111: 
  112:     if (newFen !== history[historyIndex]) {
  113:       const nextHistory = history.slice(0, historyIndex + 1);
  114:       nextHistory.push(newFen);
  115:       setHistory(nextHistory);
  116:       setHistoryIndex(nextHistory.length - 1);
  117:     }
  118:   }
  119: 
  120:   function handleUndo() {
  121:     if (historyIndex > 0) {
  122:       const prevIndex = historyIndex - 1;
  123:       const prevFen = history[prevIndex];
  124:       const fields = prevFen.split(/\s+/);
  125:       setPlacement(fields[0]);
  126:       setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  127:       setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  128:       setFenInputText(prevFen);
  129:       setHistoryIndex(prevIndex);
  130:       setValidationError("");
  131:     }
  132:   }
  133: 
  134:   function handleRedo() {
  135:     if (historyIndex < history.length - 1) {
  136:       const nextIndex = historyIndex + 1;
  137:       const nextFen = history[nextIndex];
  138:       const fields = nextFen.split(/\s+/);
  139:       setPlacement(fields[0]);
  140:       setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  141:       setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  142:       setFenInputText(nextFen);
  143:       setHistoryIndex(nextIndex);
  144:       setValidationError("");
  145:     }
  146:   }
  147: 
  148:   function handleSquareClick(row: number, col: number) {
  149:     setSelectedSquare({ row, col });
  150:     if (activeTool === "") {
  151:       // Erase tool active
  152:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, "");
  153:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  154:     } else {
  155:       // Place / Replace with active piece
  156:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, activeTool);
  157:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  158:     }
  159:   }
  160: 
  161:   function handleToolSelect(tool: string) {
  162:     setActiveTool(tool);
  163:     // If a square is already selected and user picks a piece or erase, apply to that square
  164:     if (selectedSquare) {
  165:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, selectedSquare.row, selectedSquare.col, tool);
  166:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  167:     }
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   function handleClearBoard() {
  171:     pushState(EMPTY_PLACEMENT, turn, "-");
  172:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  173:   }
  174: 
  175:   function handleStartingPosition() {
  176:     pushState(START_PLACEMENT, "w", "KQkq");
  177:     setSelectedSquare(null);
  178:   }
  179: 
  180:   function handleFlipBoard() {
  181:     setFlipped((f) => !f);
  182:   }
  183: 
  184:   function handleInvertPieces() {
  185:     const nextPlacement = flipFenPlacement(placement);
  186:     pushState(nextPlacement);
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   function handleTurnChange(newTurn: "w" | "b") {
  190:     pushState(placement, newTurn, castling);
  191:   }
  192: 
  193:   function handleFenInputChange(value: string) {
  194:     setFenInputText(value);
  195:     const trimmed = value.trim();
  196:     const fields = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
  197:     if (fields.length === 6) {
  198:       const parsed = parseFenPlacement(fields[0]);
  199:       if (parsed) {
  200:         setPlacement(fields[0]);
  201:         setTurn(fields[1] === "b" ? "b" : "w");
  202:         setCastling(fields[2] ?? "-");
  203:         setValidationError("");
  204:         if (trimmed !== history[historyIndex]) {
  205:           const nextHistory = history.slice(0, historyIndex + 1);
  206:           nextHistory.push(trimmed);
  207:           setHistory(nextHistory);
  208:           setHistoryIndex(nextHistory.length - 1);
  209:         }
  210:         return;
  211:       }
  212:     }
  213:     const val = validatePositionFen(trimmed);
  214:     if (!val.isValid) {
  215:       setValidationError(val.error || "Invalid FEN syntax.");
  216:     }
  217:   }
  218: 
  219:   // Keyboard navigation for selected square
  220:   function handleKeyDown(event: React.KeyboardEvent) {
  221:     if (!selectedSquare) return;
  222:     const { row, col } = selectedSquare;
  223:     if (event.key === "Delete" || event.key === "Backspace") {
  224:       event.preventDefault();
  225:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, "");
  226:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  227:       return;
  228:     }
  229:     const key = event.key;
  230:     if (/^[prnbqkPRNBQK]$/.test(key)) {
  231:       event.preventDefault();
  232:       const nextPlacement = setFenPiece(placement, row, col, key);
  233:       pushState(nextPlacement);
  234:       setActiveTool(key);
  235:       return;
  236:     }
  237:     if (event.key === "Escape") {
  238:       setSelectedSquare(null);
  239:     }
  240:   }
  241: 
  242:   function handleConfirmClick() {
  243:     if (validationError) {
  244:       return;
  245:     }
  246:     if (!validation.isValid) {
  247:       setValidationError(validation.error || "Cannot confirm: position is not valid.");
  248:       return;
  249:     }
  250:     onConfirm(currentFen);
  251:   }
  252: 
  253:   const pieceAssetUrl = (piece: string) => {
  254:     const color = piece === piece.toUpperCase() ? "w" : "b";
  255:     const type = piece.toLowerCase();
  256:     return `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/pieces/standalone/${color}${type}.svg`;
  257:   };
  258: 
  259:   return (

  364:             >
  365:               {Array.from({ length: 64 }, (_, index) => {

  414:                           pushState(nextPlacement);
  415:                           setSelectedSquare({ row: boardRow, col: boardCol });
  416:                         }
  417:                       } catch {
  418:                         // ignore malformed drag payload
  419:                       }
  420:                     }}

  422:                     {piece && (
  423:                       <img
  424:                         draggable={false}
  425:                         alt={PIECE_LABELS[piece]}

  427:                       />
  428:                     )}
  429:                     {visibleCol === 0 && (
  430:                       <span className={`rank-coordinate ${isLight ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>
  431:                         {rankNumber}
  432:                       </span>
  433:                     )}
  434:                     {visibleRow === 7 && (
  435:                       <span className={`file-coordinate ${isLight ? "coord-dark" : "coord-light"}`}>
  436:                         {fileLetter}
  437:                       </span>
  438:                     )}
  439:                   </button>
